How to read this report
  • Each row is one step or one test. Read it left to right: what it does, what should happen, what actually happened, then the verdict.
  • The two chips on the right are the HTTP status and the response time. green means it was what the test expected (and inside the time limit); red means it was not.
  • Click a row to see why it passed or failed. The checks and the technical details (request, reply, a curl to reproduce it) are folded underneath.
  • Three sections: Test Cases is the journey itself; Validations is every broken input thrown at it, under the step it attacks; Configurable System is what this run was set up with.
